# Domain Stash Preservation Checkpoint — 2026-10-16

Current HEAD: `1bae6115bc56bf5e1b01212774dd76877a5f64fe`. Latest stash: `ec7b3361858df86599da79e5b6dde70ccbbc3625`. Stash parent: `f856dff081373e131ebf72618120cb73e303a912`. These were read from actual refs; historical a2874/b4bf refs were not used as restoration authority.

## Scope and Verification

Source/content preservation only. No build, compile, native test, runtime, pipeline, Git index, stash, checkout, commit, worktree or goal/ticket lifecycle operation was performed. This checkpoint does not claim runtime correctness or exhaustive preservation of the remaining domain delta.

Read applicable s, s modules, FEM, Puzzle, Energy, products, repo and VS Code AGENTS files. Repository instructions were not edited. All 14 destination paths are below 256 UTF-16 code units. Each write checked current bytes against the observed HEAD-equivalent source immediately before an atomic replacement from a short ticket-generated temporary path. Temporary staging files were consumed by replacement.

## Restored Content

VS Code: all eight changed files were identical between HEAD and the stash parent (new files absent on both), so the stash source/manifest/test unit could be preserved without overwriting HEAD-specific work. This restores passive source navigation, removes IDE repository-operation handlers/action lenses/menus, retains kit diagnostics, uses argv-based process execution, and preserves the stash dashboard-ownership fixture/schema and independent TypeScript/Babel source evidence.

FEM app surface: all six changed files were also unchanged from the stash parent. Restored result-mode physical-unit projection (modal frequency versus buckling multiplier), eigenmode shape selection, portable five-case corpus/schema, both language implementations, and shared color-parser handling. The current HEAD next_id helper and its collision test were retained because the current domain callers still own that API; the stash removal belongs to a larger identifier-ownership transfer that has not been transplanted. The existing shared parse_ui_color_hex implementation was located in the current UI contract component source before restoration.

All six restored JSON documents parse. Twelve of the 14 files byte-match the stash; the two FEM files intentionally retain the HEAD helper/test. A source conflict-marker scan and git diff --check over these two units completed successfully. No tests were run.

### Concrete Unrestored Productive Deltas Located

- Dashboard repo-domain: stash adds typed DraftStart/DraftDiscard, prompted TicketReopen, explicit Analyze policy, action validation/binding, error-preserving execution and schema. HEAD changes the run_action ParsedArgs owner from crate::args to dashboard_core::args; any merge must retain that owner. Related terminal launcher/registry/daemon/control-plane changes and tests remain unmerged. The VS Code passive-navigation half is restored; dashboard feature expansion is not claimed complete.
- FEM analyses: stash recomputes combination element stress from combined displacement/member loads rather than summing non-linear scalar stresses, and introduces ResultSource identity. Related analyses/sparse additions, result-mode renderer adoption, native node/material extraction, commands and corpus changes remain unreviewed. No new crate owners were restored.
- Energy: stash adds PV Faiman/Sandia temperature, plane-of-array solar components and corrected output clipping; model mounting/thermal coefficients, authored-source chronology with actual/typical-year policy, year-spanning RunPeriod bounds, thermal/enclosure/retained mutation work and associated tests remain unmerged. Electrical, model, calendar and site source files inspected were unchanged between HEAD and stash parent; dependent constructors/contracts require a whole feature merge.
- Puzzle: stash has retained job/source custody, shared mechanisms and schema/mutation/test changes. HEAD also modifies 2D board-host, precompute fill, wasm, edit modes, snapshot and retained owners plus 3D/5D snapshots/view windows. These overlap and require semantic merging rather than bulk stash replacement.
